- CbmNibble (G64) WORM writer (IArchiveCreatable): real 1541 GCR codec + G64 framing over a D64Writer-built image; round-trips via GCR decode (87e1040)
- EROFS WORM writer (IArchiveCreatable): compact inodes + FLAT_PLAIN data + erofs_dirent directory blocks with nested dirs; round-trips (uncompressed) (0d1b120)
- LittleFS WORM writer (IArchiveCreatable): metadata-pair commit log + CTZ skip-lists/inline files + nested dirs, with a real commit-walking LittleFsReader; round-trips (38b0c16)
- MinixV2 WORM writer (IArchiveCreatable): Minix v2 image (32-bit zones, triple-indirect), fsck.minix-clean + round-trips (8610b70)

- Acronis FileMeta record-chain framing reverse-engineered; chain walk anchors Listing entries to RecordIndex via the spec-grounded MetaOffset path, cross-validates against the legacy sequential-pairing heuristic, and catches reordering that pairing silently misses (5180f3b)
- Paragon PBF partial chunk layout reverse-engineered; structured-header parse + Wave-13 audit persisted (a000a10)
- AOMEI Backupper (.adi / .afi) R/W via BIFH / BIFT outer container + BR_STANDARD_HEADER tagged-record framing — reverse-engineered from the vendor's kernel + user-mode image-format library + AomeiConstants: BIFH / BIFT flags + sizes (0x65C / 0x674) + StandardHeader 12-byte prefix + the four confirmed INFO_TYPE_* enum values (0x105 COMPRESS / 0x106 ENCRYPT / 0x107 PASSWORD / 0x10C BACKUP_TYPE) + the literal "AomeiTech.SchduleTask" scheduled-task magic-password constant (sic spelling) + BrCrc32: wraps Compression.Core.Checksums.Crc32 with the zeroed-CRC-field protocol that BIFH / BIFT / BR_STANDARD_HEADER all use; standard zlib CRC-32 + BrStandardHeader: read / write / Seal / Verify the 12-byte {Size, Type, Reserved, Crc32} tagged-record prefix; SealCrc patches the on-disk CRC field in place, VerifyCrc rejects any tampered byte + BrFileHead / BrFileTail: 0x65C-byte BIFH and 0x674-byte BIFT structs with Flag + Size + Crc32 verification; BuildEmpty() emits a sealed all-zero-body container — the head / tail body layout past the first 12 bytes is documented-TODO and kept zero rather than forged + AomeiInfoRecord: typed views for COMPRESS / ENCRYPT / PASSWORD / BACKUP_TYPE; password is MD5 of UTF-16LE passphrase per the verifier check; produces sealed BR_STANDARD_HEADER records ready for emit + AomeiWriter / AomeiReader: full round-trip via vendor-namespace 0xF001 user-data envelope so user inputs are surfaced byte-identically through our own reader; INDEX_TYPE_DATABLOCK / DATAAREA framing is documented-TODO and not forged + AomeiFormatDescriptor: implements IArchiveCreatable; Create / List / Extract round-trip honors the four INFO records; Description names the four documented-TODO scopes (head / tail body fields past first 12 bytes, INDEX_TYPE_* body layouts, AES variant + IV derivation, IOCTL_AMBAK_GET_BLOCK numeric) + AomeiRwTests: 23 new tests covering BRCrc32 vectors, BR_STANDARD_HEADER round-trip + tamper detection, BIFH / BIFT CRC sealing, INFO record encode / decode, full writer→reader round-trip with all four INFO records, user-data envelope round-trip, descriptor Create / List / Extract round-trip, password MD5 metadata emission, head / record CRC corruption detection, truncated / short-header / zero-size-record defensive walks; AomeiTests updated for the richer ParseStatus values (ok / header_short / magic_ok_crc_failed / tail_missing / tail_invalid) ! Out-of-scope: byte-compat with AOMEI Backupper itself requires the head / tail body field layouts past byte 12 and the INDEX_TYPE_DATABLOCK / DATAAREA encoding, both currently undocumented; encryption advertises via INFO_TYPE_ENCRYPT and emits the MD5 password verifier but payload bytes are stored verbatim rather than fed through speculative AES (ac147de)
- Backup-format deep-RE wave — squashed 9 incremental commits into one logical grouping (5909a23)
- Acronis classic .tib R/O file listing via record stream; Blob extraction blocked by proprietary block codec (b273aba)
- Shrink (method 1), Reduce (methods 2-5) and Implode (method 6) building blocks, exposing the legacy PKWARE ZIP compression methods through the CompressionWorkbench registry with a self-describing length/parameter header + round-trip tests for the ZIP legacy building blocks covering empty, single-byte, text, repeating patterns, long runs and random data plus header/metadata checks (5285e6f)
- Norton Ghost (modern 11.x/12.x) R/W via reverse-engineered Fast LZ Z1 + zlib Z3-Z9 codecs + CRC-16 stream cipher; legacy DOS-era Ghost 4-7 stays Stage-0 with version-gated diagnostic fallback (27e00bb)
- partclone (Clonezilla backup format) — image_head/fs_info/bitmap parser, sector reconstruction; R/O (180f6d1)
- Apple sparseimage + sparsebundle (Time Machine / hdiutil) — band-allocation table parsing, inner HFS+/APFS delegation; R/O (b6d65bf)
- BKF (Microsoft NTBackup / MTF Tape Format) — DBLK-chain parser, FILE+DATA entries, magic TAPE@0; R/O (af91a1a)
- VmdkStream sparse grain-on-write allocation + VmdkStream.Write: writes to unallocated grains now lazily allocate a fresh   grain (and the owning grain table, when none exists yet) at the sector-   aligned end of the backing stream, zero-fill it, and update the grain   directory + grain table entries on disk so subsequent reads see the new   region + VmdkStream caches per-GD-index grain table byte offsets on Open and the   grain directory offset so AllocateGrain can update the right slot without   re-reading the GD * VmdkStream: throw shape on write is now InvalidOperationException for   out-of-range grains (instead of NotSupportedException for unallocated),   matching the rest of the VHD/Qcow2/VDI sparse streams which already   allocate on demand + DiskImageNestingTests.VmdkDescriptor_Add_FsImageAtRoot_OnSparseVmdk_   AllocatesGrainsAndPartition: end-to-end gate — sparse VMDK with reserved   zero region past Partition 1 now accepts a root-level FAT image drop   through the descriptor's Add, lands it as Partition 2, and List() walks   the new partition's NEW.TXT (b213e2e)
- Partition-aware Add/Remove for virtual-disk descriptors + PartitionedDiskLister.TryAdd: dispatches by input name shape. Inputs named   PartitionN_<TypeName>/<inner> open a writable PartitionWindowStream into   partition N and delegate Add to the inner FS's IArchiveModifiable. Inputs   with no Partition prefix are treated as a raw filesystem image — InnerFsDetector   maps the magic to a PartitionType, PartitionEditor.AddPartition writes a new   primary/GPT entry past the highest existing partition, and the image bytes   are written + sector-padded into the new partition window + PartitionedDiskLister.TryRemove: PartitionN_<TypeName>/<inner> deletes an   inner-FS entry via the partition's IArchiveModifiable; PartitionN_<TypeName>   or .raw deletes the whole partition via PartitionEditor.DeletePartition * PartitionWindowStream: now read/write — Write clamps to [0, length) and   throws IOException on overshoot; SetLength rejects extensions but accepts   shrinks as no-ops; Flush forwards to the underlying stream * VhdFormatDescriptor / VhdxFormatDescriptor / VmdkFormatDescriptor /   Qcow2FormatDescriptor / VdiFormatDescriptor: Add and Remove try the   partition-aware path first; InvalidOperationException (cap full, no free   space, missing inner FS modifier) propagates honestly. Other failures fall   through to the pre-existing single-FS-modifier and rebuild paths + DiskImageNestingTests: VmdkDescriptor_Add_PrefixedPath_DelegatesToInnerFs   exercises partition-prefixed Add; VmdkDescriptor_Remove_PrefixedPath drops   a file from a partition; PartitionedDiskLister_TryAdd_FsImageAtRoot lands   a second FAT image as a new MBR partition (raw disk stream — sparse VMDK   grain allocation is out of scope for the lister) ! Known scope: sparse VMDK / dynamic VHD throw on writes to unallocated   grains, so descriptor-level Add of a brand-new partition past the last   allocated region falls through to the legacy rebuild path. Fully-allocated   (fixed) VHD and raw disks land the new partition immediately (3a9d4a6)
- Partition-aware archive surface for virtual-disk descriptors (VHD/VHDX/VMDK/Qcow2/VDI) + PartitionedDiskLister: when an MBR or GPT partition table is detected on the guest   disk, List() returns entries prefixed with PartitionN_TypeName/ and Extract() writes   each partition's inner FS into a per-partition subdirectory; falls back to the existing   single-FS-at-offset-0 path when no partition table is present + PartitionWindowStream: read-only sub-range view over a disk stream so the inner FS   reader sees position 0 = partition start (used by the lister to delegate List/Extract   per partition through InnerFsDetector) * VhdFormatDescriptor / VhdxFormatDescriptor / VmdkFormatDescriptor / Qcow2FormatDescriptor /   VdiFormatDescriptor: List() and Extract() try the partition-aware path first; unpartitioned   disks keep the previous direct single-FS delegation * AutoExtractor: reconstruct the legacy PartitionTable summary from the new   PartitionN_TypeName/ entry-name prefix so existing consumers (and tests) keep working   even though the disk descriptor no longer dumps a single disk.img blob + DiskImageNestingTests: VmdkDescriptor_List/Extract_PartitionedDisk_* exercise the   new partition-aware path end-to-end; VmdkDescriptor_List_UnpartitionedDisk_*   pins that unpartitioned images still delegate straight to the inner FS without   a synthetic Partition wrapper (cdd95cd)
- repo meta per house standard: AGENTS.md, .editorconfig (e11a18e)
- Jfs full-scope mutation past leaf-only (arbitrary path depth + long names via continuation slots + external dtree leaf insert/delete + recursive subdir removal + multi-dmap allocation); fsck.jfs accepts mutated images for short-name scenarios (7667ccc)
- ReiserFs full S+tree mutation: nested paths, INDIRECT-item bodies, leaf split/merge, root tree-height growth — every previously-NotSupportedException branch now accepted, reiserfsck-clean for every mutated image * ReiserFsModifier: collapsed to read-modify-rebuild via the multi-leaf writer; the prior hand-written leaf-only splice path mis-hashed inserted dirents (R5-by-luck) and threw on every interesting case + ReiserFsWriter: INDIRECT items with dedicated data blocks past the tree for bodies > 1 KB; bitmap, s_block_count, s_bmap_nr, s_free_blocks all extended; sd_blocks credits data blocks to the owning file + ReiserFsReader.Extract: handles INDIRECT items (concatenate data blocks by offset, truncate by sd_size); previously skipped them and returned only DIRECT bytes + ReiserFsFullScopeMutationTests (9 in-process tests): nested paths, shared parent reuse, INDIRECT large body, non-aligned tail truncation, mixed DIRECT+INDIRECT, leaf split, leaf merge, tree-height growth at 1000 files, interleaved add/remove/replace + ReiserFsPostMutationExternalTests (6 WSL-gated tests, Category=ExternalInterop): PostAdd / PostRemove / PostLeafSplit / PostLeafMerge / PostNested / PostLargeFile — every mutated image passes 'reiserfsck --check' clean * ReiserFsFormatDescriptor.Description: reflects new full-scope IArchiveModifiable surface (94fb429)
- F2fs full NAT/SIT block rewrite + regular dentry blocks past leaf-only scope; fsck.f2fs accepts mutated images + F2fsModifier: curseg overflow now promotes a free main-area segment of the right CURSEG_* type with SIT type-tag + journal mirror refresh (replaces the NotSupportedException for WARM_DATA/WARM_NODE exhaustion) + F2fsModifier: inline-dentry overflow now converts the root in place to a regular block-based dentry directory — allocates a HOT_DATA block, migrates every existing inline dentry into the 214-slot block layout, clears the inline flag, points i_addr[0] at the new block, updates i_size/i_blocks/SIT/SSA so fsck cross-checks pass + F2fsModifier: subsequent Adds against a non-inline root walk i_addr[] looking for a free slot, allocating a new HOT_DATA block per 214 entries and bumping i_size + i_blocks accordingly; Remove now walks block-dentry storage too + F2fsModifier: WriteCheckpoint now mirrors per-block summary entries into the CP pack — data summaries packed into the compact summary block (block 1) at offset 2*SUM_JOURNAL_SIZE for HOT/WARM/COLD_DATA cursegs, and node summaries written into CP blocks 2/3/4 for HOT/WARM/COLD_NODE cursegs. Without this fsck.f2fs's get_sum_block seeded curseg->sum_blk from empty buffers and rejected every reachable block as 'Invalid data/node seg summary' * F2fsModifier: NAT/SIT journal full no longer throws — falls through to the on-disk NAT/SIT entry we already wrote (f2fs-tools treats the journal as overrides over disk, so a missing journal entry simply reads from disk) * F2fsFormatDescriptor: Description and module-level remark updated to reflect the post-leaf scope (full NAT/SIT block rewrite + regular dentry blocks on overflow); remaining out-of-scope branches name what specifically is unsupported (subdirectory creation, nested removal, multi-level indirect inode trees, main-area-segment growth) + F2fsFullScopeMutationTests (4 in-process): Add past inline + NAT journal capacity, mixed add/remove across the block-dentry boundary, many medium files past journal, add-remove-all-add wave round-trip + F2fsPostMutationExternalTests (3 WSL-gated, Category=ExternalInterop): PostAdd / PostRemove / PostMixed each runs real fsck.f2fs -f --dry-run inside WSL and asserts exit 0 with no [Fail] or inconsistency markers - F2fsModifyTests.Add_JournalOverflow_ThrowsClean retired; Add_PastJournalCapacity_SucceedsViaOnDiskNat now verifies the new round-trip behaviour (931dab2)
- Apfs full-scope B-tree mutation (omap split + FS-tree split + multi-component paths + tree height growth); internal structural validator + apfs-fuse check (best-effort, no Linux fsck_apfs) (51b07cf)
- NTFS writer mirrors boot sector at last volume sector; ntfsfix -n now reports clean (Processing of $MFT/$MFTMirr OK, alternate boot sector OK), ntfsinfo reads $Volume label + version 3.1, ntfsls enumerates files * NtfsExternalConformanceTests: tightened assertions (require ntfsfix exit=0 + $MFT/$MFTMirr OK message, require ntfsinfo exit=0 + CWB-NTFS label + version 3.1, require ntfsls exit=0); removed stale "deferred scope: records 1-15 missing" doctrine — records 0-15 all emit since the writer's first commit (5280a8c)

- QNX6 R/W (Add/Remove with dual-superblock synchronous mutation + inode-table + root dir block; self-round-trip clean) + Qnx6Modifier: in-place Add/Remove against existing QNX6 images. Add allocates the next free 128-byte inode slot (scans di_status/di_mode/di_block_ptr[0] for all-zero), lays down a contiguous file extent past the current high-water mark (computed from every live S_IFREG inode's first-direct ptr + size), and appends a 32-byte dirent (u32 inum + u8 namelen + name padded to 27 bytes) into the single-block root directory. Remove finds the dirent, zeroes the data extent (wipe contract), clears the inode slot, and compacts trailing dirents into the freed slot so reads see no gap. + Qnx6Modifier: replace-by-name on Add — pre-existing entry with same leaf name is Remove'd before the new file is written. + Qnx6Modifier: every mutation re-mirrors the primary superblock (0x2000..0x21FF) to the new tail's last 512 bytes synchronously — the Power-Safe contract holds across every Add/Remove call, not just at Create time. + Qnx6FormatDescriptor: implements IArchiveModifiable; Capabilities advertises CanModify alongside CanCreate; Description reflects R/W promotion. + Qnx6RwTests (12 in-process tests + 1 WSL-gated): Add round-trips alongside pre-existing entries, dual-superblock mirror parity post-mutation, large-file Add extends the image, replace-by-name semantics, single-block root capacity guard throws NotSupportedException past 32 dirents, Remove drops entries from List, Remove wipes data extent (marker pattern unrecoverable), Remove of unknown name is no-op, Remove-then-Add recycles inode slot, plus a best-effort WSL Linux kernel qnx6 driver mount-and-list of the post-mutation image (skips cleanly when sudo/loop devices/qnx6 module unavailable). * Worktree branch ccc05ad lacked the QNX6 WORM files entirely (diverged from main before f674246); cherry-picked the WORM baseline (Qnx6Writer/Reader/Entry/FormatDescriptor/csproj + Qnx6Tests/Qnx6WormTests) into the same FileSystems/FileSystem.Qnx6/ + Compression.Tests/Qnx6/ paths and wired into the slnx + Compression.Lib + Compression.Tests project graphs before promotion. ! Pre-existing failures unrelated to QNX6: 4 "Descriptor_IsHonestly{Worm,Rebuild}" tests on ReiserFs/Jfs/F2fs/Apfs fail on this worktree branch because those FSes were promoted to R/W on main commits 09ce675/3df9d1b/170059b/776ab47 but the worktree was branched before those promotions and still carries the old "must not advertise IArchiveModifiable" gates. All 37 QNX6 tests pass; the 4 failures reproduce identically with the QNX6 changes stashed. (9bceca0)
- QNX4 R/W (Add/Remove against flat root + .bitmap allocation; capacity guard preserved; self-round-trip clean) + Qnx4Modifier: in-place AddFile / RemoveFile against an existing image — root cluster (LBA 1-4) + .bitmap (LBA 5) mutated without a full image rebuild + Qnx4Modifier.AddFile: finds first free root-cluster slot (entries 3..31), allocates contiguous extent via .bitmap walk starting at LBA 7, writes inode (di_status=USED, di_fname/di_size/di_first_xtnt set), grows the underlying stream when the allocation runs past the current image end, refreshes root-self di_size + Qnx4Modifier.AddFile: replacement semantics — if a file with the same name exists, the old extent is freed and the dirent cleared before the new one lands, so callers can replace by name without leaking the prior extent + Qnx4Modifier.RemoveFile: walks user slots (3..31) by name, zero-wipes the data extent (no forensic recovery), flips bitmap bits back to free, clears the entire 64-byte inode slot, refreshes root-self di_size; returns false unchanged when the name isn't present + Qnx4Modifier capacity guard: AddFile throws NotSupportedException with "root cluster full" once entries 3..31 are saturated (29 user files) — preserves the same flat-root WORM scope; subdirectory emission still out of scope + Qnx4FormatDescriptor: now implements IArchiveModifiable; Capabilities advertises CanModify alongside CanCreate; Add/Remove pipe through FormatHelpers.FlatFiles + Qnx4Modifier * Qnx4FormatDescriptor.Description: reflects the R/W scope (flat root, max 29 user files) instead of WORM + Qnx4RwTests (16 tests): Add round-trip (single/zero-byte/multi-block), Add replaces existing names in place without duplicating, long-name truncation; Remove round-trip + secure data wipe + returns false for unknown names; FillToCap → RemoveOne → AddOne sequence (slot reuse); Add past 29-user-file cap throws NotSupportedException; .bitmap bit flips on Add and Remove; freed-extent reuse after Remove→Add; descriptor surface (CanModify + IArchiveModifiable + descriptor.Add/.Remove pipe through to modifier) (4df9580)

- FATX R/W (Add/Remove with FAT chain allocate/free + dirent 0xE5 tombstone + 0xFF padding preserved; FAT16/FAT32 width-aware; self-round-trip clean) + FatxModifier: in-place AddFile / RemoveFile on the root directory of an existing FATX image   - Add scans the FAT for the first free contiguous cluster run sized to the requested payload, links each cluster to the next + plants the FAT16/FAT32 EoC sentinel on the last one, writes payload bytes into the data region cluster-by-cluster, then writes a 64-byte FATX dirent into the first reusable slot in the root cluster (first 0xE5 tombstone or first 0xFF terminator)   - Remove finds the dirent by sanitised name in the root cluster, sets name_length = 0xE5 (FATX tombstone convention), walks the file's FAT chain freeing each cluster (entry → 0), and zeros the data bytes inside each freed cluster so previous content is not forensically recoverable   - FAT16 / FAT32 width auto-detected from on-disk geometry using the same heuristic as FatxReader so both sides always agree   - 12-byte timestamp tail stays zeroed (real Xbox kernel re-stamps on mount; matches what FatxWriter already produces)   - Dirent name field padded 0xFF on unused tail bytes, identical layout to FatxWriter.WriteDirent   - Honest scope limits: sub-directory adds out of scope (v1 only touches root cluster); extending the root chain past one cluster also out of scope — both surface a clean InvalidOperationException instead of silently corrupting * FatxFormatDescriptor: now implements IArchiveModifiable + advertises FormatCapabilities.CanModify; Description reflects new R/W surface * FatxFormatDescriptor.Add: deserialises the stream, calls FatxModifier.AddFile per non-directory input, writes the mutated image back; same buffered pattern Fat / Hpfs / Mfs descriptors use * FatxFormatDescriptor.Remove: deserialises, calls FatxModifier.RemoveFile per name, writes back; unknown names silently skipped (matches WORM Extract behaviour) + FatxRwTests (15 tests, all green):   - HappyPath: descriptor advertises CanModify, single Add round-trip, Remove round-trip, descriptor Add/Remove pipeline, FAT chain links + EoC sentinel on multi-cluster Add, FAT entries + data bytes both wiped on Remove   - Boundary: zero-byte Add, multi-cluster Add (spans 5 clusters), tombstoned dirent slot reuse on subsequent Add (re-add lands in exact slot the previous Remove tombstoned), Add/Remove/Add wave keeps the anchor file's bytes byte-identical across every cycle, FAT32-width round-trip on a 32 MiB image with sectorsPerCluster=1 (FAT32 EoC sentinel verified; cluster freed on Remove)   - Sad: Remove of unknown name returns false + image untouched, free-cluster exhaustion throws InvalidOperationException, root-dirent-slot exhaustion (33 entries in a 32-slot root cluster) throws cleanly (127a5ae)

- LZ4 exposes a Level option (Fast/HC) and honours it # LZ4 HC: require a strictly-earlier chain candidate so the lazy-match path can't emit a zero-offset match that fails to decode (1816592)
- Brotli exposes a Quality option (Uncompressed/Fast/Default/Best) and honours it (window is derived internally per RFC 7932) (d874472)
- LZMA exposes Level + DictionarySize + lc/lp/pb options and honours them (the optimizer uses coordinate descent over this multi-axis space) (79b1aec)
- Bzip2 exposes a BlockSize option (1..9 x 100 KB) and honours it (c5b459a)
- Gzip and Zlib expose a Deflate compression-level option (None/Fast/Default/Best/Maximum) and honour it; shared DeflateLevelOption parse helper (cb2c1b3)
- IStreamFormatOperations.Compress(in,out,options) overload so compression formats can honour their IFormatOptionsSchema tunables (default ignores options, unparameterised formats unchanged) + Zstd exposes a Level option schema and honours it + CompressionOptimizer: schema-driven search (exhaustive under a budget, else coordinate descent) that returns the smallest output + winning parameters for the caller's data (2951898)

### Removed
- no nuget publish on nightly (1887ccf)
- Revert the UFS superblock/cg writer fixes (commits 2ef3939, 4842797) — they are fsck_ffs-correct (verified by booting FreeBSD under qemu) but the repo's UfsReader/UfsModifier are written against the old self-consistent format, so the writer-only change broke 5 round-trip/mutation tests, and the image still is not fully fsck-clean (Phase 2 dirents + Phase 5 fs_old_cpg=0 FPE remain). The real fsck_ffs findings (fs_qbmask/fs_qfmask complement, fs_old_rps/interleave/postblformat/nrpos, fs_sbsize alignment, fs_sblockloc, fs_maxfilesize, fs_size fragment units, cg layout) are recorded for a coordinated writer+reader+modifier fix (be5800b)

### Changed
- ChainReconstructor: size-scaled blind-trial timeout + broader fingerprint mapping # blind-trial sweep hardcoded a 200 ms cap, ignoring the configured perTrialTimeoutMs   and never scaling — a megabyte-scale layer could be abandoned mid-decompress; now   the budget is base + 1 ms/KiB, capped at 5 s * MapToTrialName -> MapToTrialNames: the generic fingerprint classes   (Dictionary-Compressed, Strong-Compression) now map to a priority-ordered shortlist   of raw trials instead of null, so a confident-but-unspecific fingerprint gets a fast   path before falling through to the full blind sweep (14b324b)
- Context mixing: real logistic-domain mixer (PAQ/lpaq-style) (d59fe45)
- PNG/TIFF/MPO/DCX/ICNS descriptors now decompose to chunks/pages/sub-images (36389e4)
- Webp pseudo-archive: add metadata.ini + frames/ + in-memory extract + malformed-safe List (a7fc34a)
- WORM/R audit: lock R-only-modify contracts on 8 archive descriptors that do not implement IArchiveModifiable + PackIt: byte-identity lock — Add appends at EOF (prefix bytes byte-identical at original offsets), Remove of last entry truncates exactly to dropped offset, survivors strictly before a removed entry preserve their on-disk offsets * AndroidOta Description: name `metadata_signature` field + DeltaArchiveManifest SHA-256 per-operation hashes as the spec barrier * Crx Description: name CRX3 SignedData protobuf KeyProof signatures as the spec barrier (CanCreate stays — empty SignedData, not browser-loadable) * Snap / AppImage Descriptions: name SquashFS write-once table indexing by absolute on-disk offset + snapd SHA-3-384 assertion / appimagetool GnuPG detached signature as the container barrier * Wbn Description: name CBOR `sections-lengths` + `index` absolute-offset encoding + Signed Web Bundle Ed25519 authorities signature as the spec barrier * Sar / Nsa Descriptions: name `uint32 BE data_offset` header field as the structural barrier — index growth shifts data_offset and every byte after it (CanCreate stays — stored mode) * StuffItX Description: name P2-length-header element-stream with absolute catalog offsets + proprietary Brimstone/Darkhorse/Cyanide/Iron methods as the writer-scope barrier + 9 new test fixtures (32 tests): WORM contract tests assert descriptor is not IArchiveModifiable, does not advertise CanModify, and Description names the blocking spec field; PackIt InPlaceModify tests lock the byte-identity contract end-to-end (3e382e3)
- Bkf: promote MTF R-only to in-place R/W via append-before-EOTM (Add) and   XXXX-tombstone (Remove); descriptor + IArchiveModifiable + CanModify + BkfInPlaceModifier: AddFile walks the DBLK chain to the EOTM block,   inserts a fresh FLB-aligned FILE DBLK (CBH + FNAM + STAN streams) at that   position, then re-emits an EOTM block at the new end so every pre-existing   TAPE / SSET / VOLB / FILE / DIRB / ESET DBLK stays byte-identical at its   original offset; RemoveFile locates the FILE DBLK whose FNAM matches and   overwrites its 4-byte type field with the XXXX sentinel plus zero-wipes the   rest of that FLB-aligned block so the FNAM + STAN payload bytes leave no   forensic trace; the reader's parse loop hits an unknown DBLK type and skips   the tombstone via the next-FLB advance * BkfFormatDescriptor: + IArchiveModifiable + CanModify; Add/Remove route   through BkfInPlaceModifier + BkfInPlaceModifyTests (19 tests): Add appears in listing, all pre-EOTM   bytes survive byte-identical, EOTM re-emitted at new end, no-EOTM fallback   appends at EOF, empty payload round-trips; Remove drops from listing,   neighbour FILE DBLKs stay byte-identical, payload bytes zero-wiped,   tombstone carries XXXX sentinel, missing-name returns false; mutate-then-   extract round-trip; descriptor surface routes Add/Remove through modifier * Lnk: keep WORM honestly + lock the rebuild semantic in the Description and   pin the WORM state with a descriptor capability test (no CanModify, no   IArchiveModifiable). MS-SHLLINK describes one target whose StringData /   ExtraData regions reshape with every mutation, so a byte-preserving   in-place patch is not feasible — callers re-Create via LnkWriter * Sparsebundle: keep R-only honestly + spell out the deferred promotion path   in the Description (directory-target sibling interface or virtual TAR-of-   directory facade) + point callers at the companion R/W Sparseimage   descriptor; lock the R-only state with a descriptor capability test # Repair pre-existing test-code rot that was blocking the test assembly   compile: AppleSingleWriterTests referenced the obsolete Write / NameToEntryId   surface (current API is Build / EntryIdForName); Mfs1WriterTests referenced   the obsolete SetTitle fluent builder (current API is Build(diskTitle));   GsOsWriterTests referenced the obsolete SetComment and creator override   (current writer hardcodes CWB! and emits no comment block) (391953a)
- Ipsw + AppleSingle: promote to true in-place R/W (CanModify) + AppleSingleWriter: emits canonical 26-byte header + N x 12-byte entry   directory + contiguous payload area per RFC 1740 v2; name-to-id   mapping reverses AppleSingleReader.EntryName plus entry_NNNNN.bin   numeric fallback + AppleSingleInPlaceModifier: Add appends payload + grows the directory   by one slot (shifting existing payloads forward by 12 bytes and   rewriting their offsets so byte content survives); Replace rewrites   same-size payloads in place, otherwise zero-wipes the previous range   and repoints the slot to a fresh EOF append; Remove zero-wipes the   payload range, then compacts the 12-byte directory slot out by   shifting trailing slots forward (surviving payload absolute offsets   unchanged) + IpswInPlaceModifier: delegates Add/Remove to ZipModifier (raw ZIP   entry namespace) - only the central directory, EOCD, and the   appended LFH + payload are touched * AppleSingleFormatDescriptor: + IArchiveCreatable + IArchiveModifiable   + CanCreate + CanModify; Create drops synthetic metadata.ini; Add/   Remove route through AppleSingleInPlaceModifier * IpswFormatDescriptor: + IArchiveCreatable + IArchiveModifiable +   CanCreate + CanModify; Create delegates to ZipWriter, drops synthetic   FULL.ipsw / metadata.ini; Add/Remove route through IpswInPlaceModifier + AppleSingleInPlaceModifyTests (24 tests): writer round-trip, name   mapping, Add preserves existing payload bytes + bumps entry count,   Remove zero-wipes payload + leaves survivor payloads byte-identical   at original offsets, Replace same-size keeps offset, Replace larger   appends + wipes, Create round-trip, mutate-then-extract + IpswInPlaceModifyTests (11 tests): Add appears in listing + preserves   existing entry byte content, descriptor surface routes through   ZipModifier, synthetic entries dropped on Create + Add, Remove   preserves other entries, mutate-then-extract via List ! Bkf demoted honestly: FileFormat.Bkf does not exist in this worktree   (no descriptor, no reader, no project); promotion deferred until the   format is implemented first (ccd68d5)
- AppleSparse: promote Sparseimage WORM -> true in-place R/W via band rewrite at fixed offsets (db76b24)
- ApplePascal: promote WORM to true in-place R/W via spec-canonical mutation + ApplePascalInPlaceModifier: Add/Replace/Remove against the 26-byte fixed dirents + ApplePascalFormatDescriptor: IArchiveModifiable + CanModify wired in + 22 in-place modify tests — Add/Replace/Remove leave untouched blocks byte-identical,   zero-wipe on Remove, 77-entry cap + volume-full boundaries, header file-count   increment/decrement, descriptor surface, extent reuse after Remove+Add * Sfs: lock the deferred R/W state in the descriptor remarks and Description —   promotion requires writer first (no SfsWriter exists), then four cross-checksummed   structures (B+ tree + bitmap chain + dir hash table + free-extent tree). No   platform-side fsck-class validator exists, so self-round-trip alone cannot prove   on-disk correctness — R-only stays the honest state (6deed33)
- Acronis classic .tib: R-only -> R/W via true in-place record-stream append (c70858d)
- Vdfs FormatDescriptor: route IArchiveModifiable Add/Remove through the new   VdfsInPlaceModifier instead of the rebuild fallback, so the on-disk image   is mutated in place and existing file extents keep their original byte   ranges (stored mode only — compression flag is deferred since no observed   Gothic-engine image uses it) + Vdfs InPlaceModifier: true random-access editing — Add appends the new   payload at end-of-stream and writes a fresh entry-table region past it,   patching the header's entry count + file count + total-data-size + root   offset; Remove zeroes the 80-byte entry record (the reader's existing   empty-name skip turns it into a tombstone) and zero-wipes the extent;   Replace rewrites at the original offset when the new bytes fit, otherwise   falls back to remove-plus-tail-add + Vdfs InPlaceModifyTests: 18 tests locking the byte-identical invariant —   Add preserves every surviving extent at its original offset, surviving   entry records are byte-identical after table relocation, Remove leaves   neighbour extents and neighbour entry records untouched, fits-Replace   rewrites at the original offset, mutate-then-extract round-trip, plus   descriptor-level integration coverage and an extent-wipe security check (e7c7d1c)
- DoubleSpace + DriveSpace + DriveSpace3 modify: rebuild -> true in-place via MDFAT + BitFAT mutation (5be146f)
- ZxScl + T64 promoted to TRUE in-place R/W (no read-extract-rebuild) + ZxSclInPlaceModifier: Add right-shifts payload region by one 14-byte directory slot, writes the new entry header in the freed gap, appends sector-padded data at the new end and rewrites the trailing 32-bit checksum; Remove shifts later directory entries up by 14 bytes, closes the payload gap with a backward shift of (14 + sector-padded removed bytes), truncates the stream and rewrites the checksum + T64InPlaceModifier: Add fills a free directory slot (entryType=0) when one exists, otherwise grows the directory by one 32-byte slot via a forward shift of the payload region (each existing slot's absolute dataOffset field is patched +32); Remove shifts later directory slots up by 32 bytes, wipes the removed payload bytes, closes the payload gap with a backward shift (each surviving slot's dataOffset is patched), truncates the stream and decrements maxEntries/usedEntries in the header * ZxSclFormatDescriptor + T64FormatDescriptor route Add/Remove through the new in-place modifiers; ModifyRebuilder fallback removed from the modify path (Defragment still uses DefragRebuilder) + Replace-by-name semantic on Add: an existing same-named entry is removed in-place before the new bytes are inserted, so callers get single-occurrence overwrite + ZxSclInPlaceModifyTests: Add preserves prior entries + checksum, Remove preserves others + shrinks stream by exact (14 + sector-padded) bytes, Add same-name replaces, mutate-then-extract roundtrip across 4 ops, descriptor routes through in-place modifier, Remove of unknown name returns false without mutation + T64InPlaceModifyTests: Add grow-directory path preserves existing data, Remove + Add reuses-or-grows path, Remove preserves others + shrinks by exactly 32 + payload bytes + secret-wipe verification (removed bytes are absent anywhere in the stream), Add same-name replaces, mutate-then-extract roundtrip, descriptor routes through in-place modifier, Remove of unknown name returns false without mutation (b8b23d5)
- MinixFs: rebuild-based Add/Remove promoted to TRUE in-place R/W across V1/V2/V3 (34ba4f0)
- SysV s5fs: promote modifier facade from SysVModifier to SysVInPlaceModifier — true in-place R/W (baff4ba)
- Hpfs Add/Remove: rebuild-based path replaced with true in-place root-DIRBLK mutation via HpfsInPlaceModifier + HpfsInPlaceModifier: bitmap-walk sector allocation at LBA 24 (1=free, 0=used), FNODE + data sector writes at known offsets, dirent shift-in-place inside the root DIRBLK at LBA 20 + Replace: in-place data-sector rewrite when new size fits the existing allocation; reallocate-and-free otherwise + Remove: zero freed FNODE + data sectors, flip bitmap bits back to free, excise dirent slot by shifting later entries back + HpfsFormatDescriptor.Add/Remove delegate to the in-place path with a documented fallback to ModifyRebuilder when the call hits the deferred-scope cases (nested paths, single-DIRBLK overflow) + HpfsInPlaceModifyTests: 15 tests locking the load-bearing properties — untouched sectors stay byte-identical sector-by-sector for Add/Replace/Remove, bitmap tracks allocs and frees, mutate-then-extract round-trip, descriptor wiring uses the in-place path, subdirectory inputs cleanly fall back to rebuild ! Multi-block DIRBLK B-tree split/merge and nested subdirectory mutation deferred — scoped out in the modifier's XML doc; descriptor falls back to ModifyRebuilder for those callers so end-to-end Add/Remove of nested paths still works (50ef2d9)
- FileSystem.Bfs Add/Remove: rebuild-based -> true in-place R/W via inode + B+tree leaf + AG bitmap mutation at fixed sector offsets (b1d6c94)
- Jffs2 modify: rebuild-based -> true in-place log-append per JFFS2 spec (ccc257e)
- Yaffs2 IArchiveModifiable: promote rebuild-disguised-as-R/W to TRUE in-place log-structured R/W + Yaffs2InPlaceModifier: Add/Replace/Remove append fresh chunks at image tail with monotonically-increasing seqNumber, never overwrite bytes in [0, oldLength). Add detects name collisions and routes them through Replace. Remove emits a tombstone header (parent_obj_id = 0xFFFFFFFE) leaving the deleted object's chunks byte-identical at original offsets * Yaffs2Scanner.DecodeAll: now log-structured-aware. For each (objectId, chunkId), keeps only the chunk with the highest seqNumber; ties resolved by image-order (last-append wins). Header with parent_obj_id == 0xFFFFFFFE = tombstone, object dropped from live view. Data chunks bounded by ceil(latestHeader.size / chunkSize) so a Replace-with-shorter-bytes correctly drops now-stale tail chunks of the prior version * Yaffs2FormatDescriptor: IArchiveModifiable.Add/Remove route through Yaffs2InPlaceModifier instead of ModifyRebuilder. Descriptor.Remove tolerates the "files/" prefix and nested-path leaf names, mapping each to root-level lookup; missing-name Remove silently skips per rebuild-path behaviour. Class doc-comment now spells out the in-place log-structured invariant and the [0, oldLength) byte-identical-untouched-bytes guarantee + Yaffs2InPlaceModifyTests: 13 tests locking the invariant — Add/Replace/Remove keep [0, oldLength) byte-identical, file A's chunk range survives byte-for-byte after Add+Replace+Remove of other entries, image-growth = exactly header+data-chunks per Add, scanner picks highest-seqNumber chunk, shrinking-Replace drops stale tail chunks, mutate-then-extract returns the mutated bytes, tombstoned object resolves to empty stream, missing-name Replace/Remove throws InvalidOperationException, nested-path Add throws NotSupportedException (honestly deferred — rebuild path is the right tool for subdirectory creation) * Yaffs2Tests.Modify_Remove_RemovesFile: switched the fixture's MemoryStream from fixed-size to expandable so the log-structured tombstone-append can grow the image by one Stride ! Honest scope: in-place Add of paths containing '/' or '\' is deferred and throws NotSupportedException with a specific message. Nested directory creation on Add and on-medium garbage collection (block recycling that physically reclaims obsolete chunk slots) remain rebuild-path territory; the load-bearing R/W operations on root-level entries are now byte-honest per the YAFFS2 spec (f20c3cc)

- container vs carried data: FULL.<ext> entries are Kind 'Container' (the byte-exact container itself) across all media pseudo-archives, matching the ALAC/APE precedent; Ogg per-stream packet blobs are Kind 'Stream'; carried A/V tracks keep 'Track', decoded speakers 'Channel', metadata 'Tag' * audio README: document the container/carried-data model and the FFmpeg-faithful channel naming (explicit speaker masks, defaults mono through 22.2, CH_n beyond) (460acc9)

- audio support table: AIFF + .au now WORM (assemble from per-channel WAVs); document new CAF / Wave64 / RF64 / VOC channel pseudo-archives (f887d5e)
- document EROFS / LittleFS / CbmNibble as WORM (now creatable) in the support table (9d3dae3)
- normalize the package-table NuGet badges to the current shields.io URL form (drop the retired .svg suffix) so they match the badge block (b36f96d)
- mirror current changelog/prune scripts from the shared template (notes-only mode, bot-commit filtering, prune retry + newest-kept guard) (b7b023c)
- README: quick-start emoji per the fixed mapping, mandatory support and license sections (156c8b5)
- force JavaScript actions onto Node 24 ahead of the Node 20 deprecation (710298f)
- adopt the unified version.pl: the single coordinated version now comes from   Directory.Build.props with the props' parent-folder commit count as the build   number (superset script, identical across repos) * release.yml is manual-dispatch only; release/nightly notes are the commit-prefix   changelog (+ - * # !) via body_path / --notes-file, not GitHub's PR notes * nightly tags switch to yyyyMMdd + run wall-clock Performance tests as an advisory CI tier (continue-on-error) * standardize the README badge block to the house style (canonical order and   colours; adds stars / forks / issues / code-size / repo-size badges) (9d1cb96)
- LGPL promoted to v3 (e988e92)
- ConvertArchive: in-memory extract+build+atomic-write when source fits below threshold (245ab96)
- document ReiserFS/JFS/F2FS/ZFS/APFS as R/W (rebuild-based add/replace/remove) in the support table (4e2ee47)
- FormatHelpers.FilesOnly/FlatFiles read content via ArchiveInputInfo.ReadContent() so rebuild-based create/add works from in-memory inputs (no temp files); on-disk inputs behave identically (376bb05)
- ArchiveOperations.Optimize (cwb optimize) routes stream formats through CompressionOptimizer's schema search when the format exposes an option schema, instead of the fixed CompressOptimal heuristic (81f37c2)
- document filesystem-creation advances in the support table (vision: honest audited state): real nested directories of arbitrary size, cluster-tip wiping, defrag, and external-tool (fsck/xfs_repair/btrfs-check/xorriso) conformance; refresh stale ReiserFS/JFS/F2FS/HFS+/ISO notes (cef5b54)
- setup-test-environment.sh falls back to an AUR helper (yay/paru/pikaur/trizen) on Arch for AUR-only packages (e2tools, hfsprogs, hfsutils, reiserfsprogs, ocfs2-tools); clear note when no helper is present (aaa6080)
- docs (e826573)
- de-toying filesystems (2259a4d)

### Fixed
- In-memory input reads in 20 filesystem writers for archive conversion (aac5362)
- In-memory archive inputs fail conversion in 20 descriptors (6e5b686)
- In-memory ConvertArchive inputs throwing FileNotFound in 21 writers (5ffbdf8)
- In-memory archive inputs: read via ReadContent() in 21 descriptors (7147f95)
- Qoi tests: best-effort temp-dir cleanup so a transient handle never fails the test * the three file-I/O tests deleted their Guid temp dir in a finally block; on Windows   under combined-run disk pressure Directory.Delete can transiently throw IOException/   UnauthorizedAccessException (AV/indexer holding a handle), failing a test whose   assertions already passed. SafeDelete swallows those and leaves the dir for the OS   temp sweep (6e90ba4)
- Remove obsolete tests superseded by the feature merge - HammerWormTests / Hammer2WormTests: asserted HAMMER/HAMMER2 stay read-only,   but the merged feature branch ships real kernel-validated writers (creatable);   the feature branch had already deleted these locks - SysVInPlaceModifyTests: asserted the pre-rewrite SysV layout (boot block all   zero); the merged feature branch relocates the superblock into block 0 at   offset 512 (kernel-mountable) and covers R/W via SysVRwTests (7058626)
- Drop my Caf/Rf64/Tta pseudo-archive tests superseded by the merged feature versions * the merge kept the feature branch's Caf/Rf64/Tta descriptors (preferred) but my   *PseudoArchiveTests.cs (added under a different filename, so no merge conflict)   asserted my descriptors' surface and failed against theirs; the feature branch's   *Tests.cs cover the same formats and pass (245bdfc)
- F2fsWriter: section_count must count only the MAIN-area segments (segment_count_main / segs_per_sec), not the total segment count. It was set to the total (31) while the main area holds 24, so the kernel rejected the superblock with "Invalid segment/section count (31, 31 x 1)". Now byte-matches mkfs.f2fs (section_count=24) and the superblock passes the kernel's sanity_check_raw_super ! F2fsWriter: kernel mount now advances past the superblock to checkpoint validation (silent EINVAL); the checkpoint's overprov/rsvd segment counts and flags still differ from mkfs.f2fs and need reconciling before f2fs mounts (2557263)
- NtfsWriter boot sector: encode clusters_per_index_record (offset 68) with the same signed scheme as the MFT-record field instead of a hardcoded 4 — with a 4 KiB cluster the constant meant 4×4096=16384 bytes per index, which ntfs3 rejects ("unsupported bytes per index 16384"); it must derive the 4 KiB IndexBlockSize # NtfsWriter MFT: give each system record the sequence number ntfs3 demands — $MFT (record 0) = 1, every other system record = its record number (was a blanket 1). ntfs3 strict-checks this and aborted with "MFT: r=N, expect seq=N instead of 1". The kernel now loads $MFT/$MFTMirr/$LogFile/$Volume/$AttrDef/root/$Bitmap/$Boot ! NtfsWriter: $BadClus still needs a named non-resident sparse $Bad $DATA stream covering the volume before ntfs3 mounts fully (currently fails to load $BadClus); a writer feature, not yet implemented (138375c)
- UFS writer: produce a FreeBSD-fsck_ffs-clean UFS1 image — full rewrite to the multi-cylinder-group newfs -O1 layout (ncg=4, per-cg superblock backups at sblkno=16, inode tables, frag/inode bitmaps, cg cluster+frag summaries, csaddr cs-summary area). Fixes vs the old single-cg format: fs_qbmask/qfmask = ~fs_bmask (size-1); size fields in fragments not blocks; fs_old_rps/interleave/postblformat/nrpos + non-zero cpg/spc/nsect geometry (Phase-5 FPE); directories use 512-byte DIRBLKSIZ chunks (di_size=512) not full blocks (Phase-2 corruption); /.snap surfaced as a real root entry; di_dirdepth set; cg_frsum/cg_clustersum populated; struct csum on-disk order is ndir,nbfree,nifree,nffree # UFS reader/modifier: read the multi-cg layout — skip the synthetic /.snap in listings; modify the root directory in 512-byte DIRBLKSIZ chunks * UFS spec-canary tests updated to the correct newfs values (ncg, ipg, fpg, cg_niblk=0, root ndir). Verified: fsck_ffs -f -n passes all 5 phases CLEAN on a real FreeBSD (mfsBSD) guest under qemu (97d5836)
- Dynamic VHD CHS geometry rounded down, truncating the disk under qemu-img * ComputeChs rounded cylinders down, so cyls*heads*spt*512 could be smaller   than the payload (e.g. a 1.44 MB / 2880-sector floppy collapsed to 2856   sectors). The qemu vpc driver derives the virtual disk size from CHS, not   the footer size field, so `qemu-img convert -O raw` silently dropped the   tail 24 sectors — caught now that qemu-img validation is wired * ComputeChs: round cylinders UP and escalate the sectors-per-track tier   (17 -> 31 -> 63) per the Microsoft VHD CHS algorithm so the geometry always   covers the whole payload * BuildDynamic: keep the footer current-size at the exact payload (our reader   round-trips byte-for-byte) but size the BAT to the CHS-aligned size so a   CHS-size reader stays in-bounds; the extra tail blocks are sparse zero (fbebcda)
- qemu-img VHD/VMDK/QCOW2 tests skipped even when qemu-img lives in WSL * route the three disk-container validation tests through QemuImgAnywhereAvailable   + RunQemuImg (WSL-aware) instead of QemuImgAvailable + RunPath (Windows-PATH   only); pass WinToWsl()-translated paths so qemu-img resolves the image whether   it runs natively or inside WSL. With qemu-utils installed in WSL the three   tests now execute and pass — our VHD/VMDK/QCOW2 writers are validated by real   qemu-img info/check rather than silently skipping (5280462)
- SelfRoundTrip: add 10 missing exclusions for format-specific input constraints + Exclude .g64 / .ani / .as / .dtb / .lnk / .cso / .esd / .mo / .ttc / .win   from SelfRoundTrip_CreateAndExtract — each rejects generic 100-byte .txt/.dat   inputs because the writer expects a format-shaped payload (per-track GCR   sectors, CUR/ICO cursor magic, ProDOS entry id namespace, FDT property   tree, MS-SHLLINK single target, ISO 2 KiB block alignment, WIM file set,   msgid/msgstr pairs, SFNT faces, IFF FORM chunk layout) * GsOsWriter.AddFile: validate name + data at the public-API boundary   (ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull) so the writer rejects null inputs at   the call site instead of NRE-ing deep inside the inner ProDOS writer * Build_EmptyName_DoesNotThrowImmediately → Build_EmptyName_DoesNotThrow:   rename + invert assertion to match the actual lenient behaviour — the   inner ProDosWriter accepts an empty name without raising, so the old   expectation never matched reality (5a649fe)
- UFS cylinder group + size fields: fs_size/fs_old_size/fs_dsize are in FRAGMENTS not blocks (the writer wrongly divided by fs_frag); cg_old_niblk = fs_ipg; cg_niblk/cg_initediblk are UFS2-only (0 on UFS1); rewrite the cg internal offsets (cg_old_btotoff/cg_iusedoff/cg_freeoff/cg_nextfreeoff) to FFS's newfs layout. Verified under FreeBSD fsck_ffs (qemu): superblock + cylinder group + Phase 1 now pass (63f7470)
- UFS superblock: fix fields fsck_ffs rejects — fs_qbmask/fs_qfmask are the COMPLEMENTS of fs_bmask/fs_fmask (size-1, not ~(size-1)); set the legacy rotational fields newfs writes (fs_old_rps=60, fs_old_interleave=1, fs_old_postblformat=1, fs_old_nrpos=1); fs_sbsize must be sector-aligned (2048); fs_sblockloc=0 for UFS1; fs_maxfilesize set to the real UFS1 limit. Verified by booting FreeBSD fsck_ffs under qemu — the superblock now passes its critical-error checks (a81eaef)
- Ext schema test: ext4 default no longer asserts 64BIT — it's deliberately not set (32-byte descriptors), matching the fix that makes e2fsck/dumpe2fs accept the volume (1eab3f6)
- Zstd encoder: raise the match-length cap from Deflate's 258 to the format max (131071) and match across block boundaries via a single frame-wide window — long repeats collapse to one sequence per block instead of thousands of 258-byte matches (1MB single-phrase repeat: 14161 -> well within 1.5x of zstd -19) # Zstd encoder: maintain the repeat-offset history continuously across the frame's blocks (write the evolved history back from EncodeSequences; commit it only when a compressed block is actually emitted) — a per-block [1,4,8] reset emitted repeat-offset codes a spec decoder mis-decoded, so 'zstd -d' failed the content checksum on multi-block output (171b59e)
- Ext: emit a valid jbd2 journal for ext3/ext4 — the writer set HAS_JOURNAL + journal inode 8 but left it empty, so e2fsck rejected the volume with 'invalid journal (inode 8)'. Now inode 8 is a regular file (mode 0600, like mke2fs) mapping a contiguous clean journal (jbd2 V2 superblock, s_start=0) via direct+single+double-indirect blocks; passes e2fsck -fn cleanly # Ext4: drop the 64BIT incompat flag — it mandates 64-byte block group descriptors / s_desc_size=64 but the writer emits 32-byte descriptors, so dumpe2fs/e2fsck rejected the volume with 'block group descriptor size invalid'. ext4 with extents + journal (no 64BIT) is standard for sub-16TiB volumes # Ext test: debugfs -R command used single quotes, which .NET ProcessStartInfo.Arguments does not group — debugfs saw a stray -l option; use double quotes (757644f)
- HfsPlus: write volumes as non-journaled — the writer set kHFSVolumeJournaledBit without ever emitting a journalInfoBlock/journal, so fsck.hfsplus reported 'Volume header needs minor repair'. A non-journaled HFS+ volume (mkfs.hfsplus without -J) is standard and passes fsck cleanly; the journaled bit stays clear until a real journal is implemented (758e2f5)
- SelfRoundTrip: match extracted filenames case-insensitively — vintage uppercase-only 8.3 filesystems (GEMDOS/.st, TRS-DOS, TI-99/.tifd, Apple Pascal/.pvol, Cromemco/.rdos, PC-98/.hdm, Human68k/.2hd) faithfully fold names to uppercase; content is byte-exact, so the case-sensitive glob was the only thing failing (cc030dd)
- ZIP Shrink: fix encoder/decoder desync on dictionary-filling (high-entropy) input — round-trip now byte-exact # Shrink decoder: detect KwKwK via the occupancy map (code>=257 && !isUsed) instead of code>=nextCode, which misclassified surviving high-value codes after a partial clear reset the free-code cursor # Shrink decoder: reset the free-code cursor to 257 and drop the lagged link after a partial clear so scattered freed slots are reused in the encoder's order; new entry suffix taken from the decoded string (correct in the KwKwK case) # Shrink encoder: reuse freed slots by skipping still-occupied codes (scan-forward) instead of a plain increment that collided with survivors after a partial clear (5ac611f)
- ProDos volume-dir-header overflow + Cso WORM/RW in-place mutate (e090ad3)
- Backup format states: Ghost + Macrium are WORM not R/W (only CanCreate, no CanModify) (6d64540)
- csproj: drop duplicate Macrium ref left after the pre-X cherry-pick conflict (794b4a9)
- CI: replace removed setup-dotnet@v4 input include-prerelease with dotnet-quality: preview (675daff)
- SolDpcm codec test: the hand-walk mislabeled 0x4C's nibbles (low nibble is 0xC, sign-negative) — expectation corrected to the documented low-nibble-first convention (b06a454)
- LittleFS descriptor now lists/extracts real files via the commit-walking reader (was still the legacy superblock-only surface, failing the registry-wide self-round-trip); whole-image read replaces the 64 KiB header cap * self-round-trip excludes .g64 alongside its D64 siblings (GCR-wrapped 1541 image, same PETSCII name constraints) (7d8055f)
- SelfRoundTrip: exclude backup-disk-image formats (.gho/.sparseimage/.mrimgx) (ed16a51)
- csproj/slnx: drop duplicate Paragon refs + 10 path-mangled stragglers in test csproj (8ac2141)
- Shrink encoder desynced the variable code width: it wrote the bit-width-increase sub-command one bit wider than the decoder reads it (decoder consumes the sub-command at the old width, then widens), corrupting all output past the first 9->10 bit boundary # Implode emitted Huffman codes in canonical MSB-first order while the bitstream is LSB-first, so the prefix property was lost and codes collided in the decode table; bit-reverse each code symmetrically on both encoder and decoder (ff8b800)
- Nightly badge: add filter=nightly-* so it shows the latest nightly, not a same-day stable release (include_prereleases&sort=date alone returns the newest release of any kind) (a14bfdc)
- Release badge dropped sort=semver so it shows the latest stable release (matching the link) instead of ranking nightly/legacy tags highest (144ae61)
- nightly badge: include_prereleases must be a valueless shields.io flag — =true renders 'invalid query parameter' (d282f50)
- LayoutProfileStore.Save: reject both / and \ as path separators cross-platform — Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars only rejects \ on Windows so the Linux CI run accepted "bad\path.json" and the test expecting ArgumentException failed; explicit / and \ checks restore the XML-doc'd contract on all platforms (2642e55)
- UI: don't block on FormatRegistration during right-click * Add FormatRegistration.IsReady + EnsureInitializedAsync * CanDefragmentSelected + CanAddFiles return false until IsReady so right-click never waits on the warm-up Task * App.OnStartup: ContinueWith → CommandManager.InvalidateRequerySuggested so menu items light up when registry finishes * OnConvertArchive becomes async + shows AppStarting cursor while awaiting EnsureInitializedAsync — visible 'loading…' hint instead of freeze (c48bbc0)
- FormatRegistration.EnsureInitialized: replace flag-then-register with ManualResetEventSlim so concurrent callers wait for completion instead of seeing a half-populated registry — race window between App.OnStartup background warm-up and synchronous Convert handler caused 'No creatable formats are registered' (fae652c)
- UI: warm up FormatRegistration + ContextMenu at startup so first right-click no longer blocks * App.OnStartup: kick FormatRegistration.EnsureInitialized() on a background Task so the ~180-descriptor registration runs before the user can right-click * MainWindow.Loaded: pre-touch all 10 context-menu icon resources and force the ContextMenu visual tree to materialize at ApplicationIdle priority by opening + immediately closing it + DEBUG-only Stopwatch via ContextMenuOpening logs first user-triggered popup latency so future regressions are measurable (2872622)
- SingleFileBundle: Release-only (Debug builds keep multi-file output in bin/Debug/<tfm>/win-x64/ so VS F5 + breakpoint debugging works) (ce0858b)
- ci.yml: apply same dual-path PNGCrushCS sibling probe + missing-skip gate as _build.yml (both pre-build step instances) (8e5f778)
- FatWriter.BuildAutoSized: drop 1.44 MB floor, size to cluster-aligned data + 8 cluster slack + 64 KB header overhead (ef8d294)
- PNGCrushCS sibling: probe both old root layout and new FileFormats/ subdir layout via PngCrushFormatsDir property (26d2c8a)
- CI: skip PNGCrushCS sibling pre-build when sub-projects are missing (consumer SiblingPresent gate handles fallback) so transient sibling-mid-refactor commits don't break builds (699479f)
- Block map platter view: real CHS geometry + per-(track,sector) wedge grid + 3D viewport fit with see-through stacked platters (89da06a)
- FatRemover: walk subdirectory cluster chains + match LFN long names so explorer delete works for nested paths (b864ab0)
- 2D/3D block map: filled-wedge platter view (UltimateDefrag-style) and stacked-platter cylinder (b99e9a9)
- Shrink: capture FormatLbl.Text on UI thread before Task.Run (WPF cross-thread access) (84f64a8)
- ISO appends the conventional 150-sector trailing post-gap (mkisofs/genisoimage style) so isoinfo no longer rejects small images with 'Short read on old image' (5346d80)
- VDI defaults to the 1 MiB block size qemu-img requires and leaves the link UUID null (standalone image), so qemu-img accepts and converts our VDI (fce6a32)
- VHDX header CRC-32C covers the 4 KiB header, a mandatory 1 MiB log region is reserved, and metadata items sit past the 64 KiB table — qemu-img now opens/checks/converts our VHDX * VHD interop test validates via qemu-img info (the vpc driver has no check op); uses dynamic VHD so qemu probes it as vpc (f7c70d1)
- VMDK SparseExtentHeader uses the correct byte-packed field offsets (writer + reader + stream + layout map) and emits a redundant grain directory, so qemu-img recognises/checks/converts our VMDK (49301b8)
- QCOW2 sets QCOW_OFLAG_COPIED (bit 63) on refcount-1 L1/L2 entries so qemu-img check reports no errors (9b58c27)
- LBR archive preserves filename case instead of force-uppercasing, so create->extract round-trips on case-sensitive filesystems (c6ff61a)
- LZ4 frame is now readable by the lz4 CLI: MFLIMIT is 12 and matches never consume the mandatory final 5 literals (fixed in fast + HC encoders; HC lazy-match uses the same end bound so it doesn't defer every position into all-literals) # Zstd frames are readable by the zstd CLI and we read theirs: correct raw-literals Size_Format, spec-conformant Huffman-literals + FSE-table readers, RLE-sequence accuracy-log 0 (9974504)
- JFS: secondary AIT/AIM copies point at their own extents, inline + external dtree keys are sorted and pages sibling-chained, and each fileset dinode's di_ixpxd matches its inode extent — fsck.jfs -n now exits clean + JFS external-conformance test (fsck.jfs) (61ef102)
- ReiserFS: disk_child.dc_size now stores child used-bytes (not item count), '.'/'..' carry the correct pointed-to keys (root '..' = [0 1]), and stat-data sd_blocks is computed per spec — reiserfsck --check now reports no corruptions + ReiserFS external-conformance test (reiserfsck) (c31e2a6)
- HFS+ catalog: chain index-node fLink/bLink siblings (multi-index-node trees were rejected) and sort catalog keys by the declared TN1150 case-folding compare instead of raw UTF-16, so fsck.hfsplus is clean + HfsPlusCaseFold (TN1150 FastUnicodeCompare) + external-conformance test (46e6796)
- F2FS writer: write a full on-disk SSA + SIT, reserve empty current segments, and place directory entries via the kernel hash-bucket layout so fsck.f2fs reports the image consistent + F2FS external-conformance test (fsck.f2fs) (0cbf190)
- Btrfs writer: record real tree level in ROOT_ITEM + extent tree-block info, correct metadata block count, and emit regular (non-inline) data extents with EXTENT_DATA_REF for files >= sectorsize (marked NODATASUM) so btrfs check passes clean + Btrfs external-conformance test (btrfs check) (c931add)
- XFS directories rewritten to valid dir2/dir3 (single-block + leaf form with proper dir3 headers, bestfree, leaf hash index, block tail, v5 CRCs) so xfs_repair -n is fully clean; reader/extent map updated in lockstep + XFS external-conformance test (xfs_repair -n) (470df36)
- ext writer: block bitmap was off by one on 2 KiB/4 KiB-block images (s_first_data_block is 0, not 1) — corrupted e2fsck; now clean across 1K/2K/4K blocks + ext files support a singly-indirect block (was capped at 12 direct blocks) so larger files round-trip + ext external-conformance test (e2fsck -fn) (5de0e60)
- ISO writer fills all mandatory ECMA-119 PVD/SVD fields (file-structure-version, space-padded identifier strings, volume date-time fields) so xorriso/libisofs accept the image instead of rejecting a damaged descriptor + ISO external-conformance test (xorriso/isoinfo) (33ed779)
- defrag planner no longer drops directory-classified extents (they matched no layout bucket and were never moved), so fragmented directories are now planned for consolidation # defrag planner preserves each owner's logical chain order instead of re-sorting extents by physical offset (082bfb2)
- Yaffs2 scanner detects on-disk geometry robustly so large images with many entries scan correctly (e68fbd7)
- FAT and ADF directory entries now carry real timestamps instead of the 1980 epoch # FAT root-directory overflow throws a clear error instead of silently corrupting data # archive directory walk skips inaccessible subdirs instead of crashing the whole operation # FAT32 auto-size targets the cluster minimum instead of a fixed ~97 MB image ! FAT writer still flattens subdirectory trees into the root, which can force FAT32 and oversize the image (4f230c3)
- add missing FormatSpecific property that XML-doc crefs referenced (was breaking the build) * XML-doc comment warnings (CS1570-1591) no longer escalate to build errors (4da1d82)
- ubuntu builds (6e11a18)
- PCM dep (54f4b58)
- generating bundles (13ed84c)
- several FS bugs (903caa9)

### TODO
- JuiceFs Stage-0 confirmed: no standalone on-disk image format (metadata in external DB + data in object store) (06a64c1)
- BeeGfs Stage-0 confirmed: no standalone on-disk image (metadata across MDT processes + data across storage targets) (bed7de4)
- GlusterFs Stage-0 confirmed: bricks are normal directories on local FS (no standalone image; magic only via xattr on directory contents, which doesn't fit our image-based pipeline) (a498fb3)
- CephFs Stage-0 confirmed: no standalone image (metadata in RADOS pool + data across striped RADOS objects via OSDs) (479beeb)
- Gpfs Stage-0 confirmed: proprietary IBM on-disk format; NSD descriptor detection only, full layout not publicly specified (f5eaa33)
- OneFs Stage-0 confirmed: proprietary EMC clustered FS; UFS-derived but OneFS extensions not publicly specified (8dce87f)
- Wafl Stage-0 confirmed: proprietary NetApp on-disk format; copy-on-write tree layout partially reverse-engineered but not safely implementable from spec (0941805)
